   36  FORM fetch_layout_texts.
   37
   38    SELECT * FROM zfi_pnl_glf4 INTO TABLE it_prod_f4 WHERE prod_proj NE '
       '.
   39    SELECT * FROM zfi_pnl_glf4 INTO TABLE it_oth_f4 WHERE oth_proj NE ' '.
   40
   41    IF it_prod_f4 IS NOT INITIAL.
   42      SORT it_prod_f4 BY prod_proj.
   43      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM it_prod_f4 COMPARING prod_proj.
   44      SORT it_prod_f4 BY text_no.
   45    ENDIF.
   46
   47    IF it_oth_f4 IS NOT INITIAL.
   48      SORT it_oth_f4 BY oth_proj.
   49      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM it_oth_f4 COMPARING oth_proj.
   50      SORT it_oth_f4 BY text_no.
   51    ENDIF.
   52
   53  ENDFORM."FETCH_LAYOUT_TEXTS

  118        CLEAR sy-ucomm.
  119        MESSAGE 'Fill in all required entry fields' TYPE 'E'.
  120
  121      ENDIF.

  123      IF p_company IS NOT INITIAL.
  124        PERFORM fetch_comp_code.
  125      ENDIF.
  126    ENDIF.
  127
  128  ENDFORM."INITAIL_CHK
  129
  130  FORM fetch_comp_code.
  131
  132    REFRESH it_comp[].
  133    REFRESH it_return[].
  134    CLEAR p_comp.
  135
  136    REFRESH lt_dynpfields[].
  137    ls_dynpfields-fieldname = 'P_COMPANY'.
  138    APPEND ls_dynpfields TO lt_dynpfields.
  139    CLEAR ls_dynpfields.
  140
  141    CALL FUNCTION 'DYNP_VALUES_READ'
  142      EXPORTING
  143        dyname               = sy-repid
  144        dynumb               = sy-dynnr
  145      TABLES
  146        dynpfields           = lt_dynpfields
  147      EXCEPTIONS
  148        invalid_abapworkarea = 1
  149        invalid_dynprofield  = 2
  150        invalid_dynproname   = 3
  151        invalid_dynpronummer = 4
  152        invalid_request      = 5
  153        no_fielddescription  = 6
  154        invalid_parameter    = 7
  155        undefind_error       = 8
  156        double_conversion    = 9
  157        stepl_not_found      = 10
  158        OTHERS               = 11.
  159
  160    READ TABLE lt_dynpfields INTO ls_dynpfields WITH KEY fieldname = 'P_CO
       MPANY'.
  161    IF ls_dynpfields IS NOT INITIAL.
  162      p_company = ls_dynpfields-fieldvalue.
  163    ENDIF.
  164
  165    SELECT txtcc bukrs FROM zfi_pnl_proj INTO TABLE it_comp WHERE company
       = p_company.
  166    SORT it_comp BY bukrs.
  167    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM it_comp COMPARING bukrs.
  168
  169    READ TABLE it_comp INTO DATA(wa_comp2) INDEX 1.
  170    IF wa_comp2 IS NOT INITIAL.
  171      p_comp = wa_comp2-bukrs.
  172    ENDIF.
  173
  174  ENDFORM.

  470  FORM fill_celltab_quant USING VALUE(p_mode) VALUE(io_flag2)
        ""making field as input field or output only
  471                    CHANGING pt_celltab TYPE lvc_t_styl.
  472
  473    DATA : ls_celltab TYPE lvc_t_styl WITH HEADER LINE.
  474
  475    CLEAR l_mode.
  476
  477    IF p_mode EQ 'RW'.
  478      l_mode = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_enabled.
  479    ELSE.
  480      l_mode = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.
  481    ENDIF.
  482
  483
  484    ls_celltab-fieldname = 'DATA'.
        "Column 1 - Field names
  485    ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.
  486    INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.
  487
  488    ls_celltab-fieldname = 'BBL'.                                        "
       Cloumn 2 - Oil(MMBBL)
  489    IF io_flag2 CA '1'.
  490      ls_celltab-style = l_mode.
  491    ELSE.
  492      ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.
  493    ENDIF.
  494    INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.
  495
  496    ls_celltab-fieldname = 'MMSCM'.
        "Columm 3 - Gas(MMBTU)
  497    IF io_flag2 CA '2'.
  498      ls_celltab-style = l_mode.
  499    ELSE.
  500      ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.
  501    ENDIF.
  502    INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.
  503
  504  *COLUMN no.4 to 9 are Output only and visible only in mode Display,Appro
       ve and Freeze.
  505    ls_celltab-fieldname = 'MMBBL'.
  506    ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.
  507    INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.
  508
  509    ls_celltab-fieldname = 'MMT'.
  510    ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.
  511    INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.
  512
  513    ls_celltab-fieldname = 'TON'.
  514    ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.
  515    INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.
  516
  517    ls_celltab-fieldname = 'MMBTU'.
  518    ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.
  519    INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.
  520
  521    ls_celltab-fieldname = 'MMBOE'.
  522    ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.
  523    INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.
  524
  525    ls_celltab-fieldname = 'BCM'.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
27.09.26 OCP      Include MZFIPLF01                             Page     13
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  526    ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.
  527    INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.
  528
  529  ENDFORM. "fill_celltab_quant

        '        '3'     ,

  556  FORM alv_fcat_quant USING  VALUE(p_011)   ""Append field catalog table
  557                             VALUE(p_012)
  558                             VALUE(p_013)

  564    ls_fcat-outputlen   = p_014.

  570
  571  FORM cal_quant.   "calculate Quant Info values
  572
  573    IF pnl NE 'PRO_ANA'.
  574      PERFORM conv_fact_chk.
  575      IF NOT grid1 IS INITIAL.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
27.09.26 OCP      Include MZFIPLF01                             Page     14
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  576        CALL METHOD grid1->check_changed_data.
  577      ENDIF.
  578    ENDIF.
  579
  580    DATA lv_msg1 TYPE string .
  581
  582    IF mode EQ 'CRE'.
  583      CLEAR : wa_final_qi_o,wa_final_qi_g,wa_final_conv.
  584    ENDIF.
  585
  586  *moving input parameters to Oil, Gas & Conversion table
  587    wa_final_conv-company = wa_final_qi_g-company = wa_final_qi_o-company
       = p_company.
  588    wa_final_conv-bukrs   = wa_final_qi_g-bukrs   = wa_final_qi_o-bukrs
       = p_comp.
  589    wa_final_conv-pro_nam = wa_final_qi_g-pro_nam = wa_final_qi_o-pro_nam
       = p_proj.
  590    wa_final_conv-fisyear = wa_final_qi_g-fisyear = wa_final_qi_o-fisyear
       = p_year.
  591    wa_final_conv-zperiod = wa_final_qi_g-zperiod = wa_final_qi_o-zperiod
       = s_period_low.
  592
  593    wa_final_conv-bbl_mmbbl   = bbl_mmbbl.
  594    wa_final_conv-bbl_mmt     = bbl_mmt.
  595    wa_final_conv-ton_bbl     = ton_bbl.
  596
  597    wa_final_conv-mmscm_mmbtu = mmscm_mmbtu.
  598    wa_final_conv-mmscm_mmboe = mmscm_mmboe.
  599    wa_final_conv-mmscm_bcm   = mmscm_bcm  .
  600
  601    "variables for calc.
  602    DATA: prf_oil_o(15)  TYPE c, sh_ent_o(15) TYPE c, trl_flr_o(15) TYPE c
       , atrl_flr_o(15) TYPE c, int_con_o(15) TYPE c,
  603          prf_oil_g(15)  TYPE c, sh_ent_g(15) TYPE c, trl_flr_g(15) TYPE c
       , atrl_flr_g(15) TYPE c, int_con_g(15) TYPE c,
  604
  605          net_prd_o(15)  TYPE c, opn_sto_o(15) TYPE c, clo_sto_o(15) TYPE
       c, chg_inv_o(15) TYPE c,
  606          net_prd_g(15)  TYPE c,
          chg_inv_g(15) TYPE c.
  607
  608    DATA : spl_flg TYPE c.
  609
  610
  611  *check for any special characters
  612    IF pnl NE 'PRO_ANA'.
  613      LOOP AT it_quant INTO DATA(wa_quant_c).
  614
  615        IF wa_quant_c-bbl CA lv_spl.
  616          CLEAR sy-ucomm.
  617          spl_flg = 'X'.
  618          MESSAGE 'Special character in amount field is not allowed' TYPE
       'W' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
  619        ENDIF.
  620        IF wa_quant_c-mmscm CA lv_spl.
  621          CLEAR sy-ucomm.
  622          spl_flg = 'X'.
  623          MESSAGE 'Special character in amount field is not allowed' TYPE
       'W' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
  624        ENDIF.
  625
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
27.09.26 OCP      Include MZFIPLF01                             Page     15
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  626        CLEAR wa_quant_c.
  627      ENDLOOP.
  628    ENDIF.
  629
  630    IF ton_bbl EQ '0.000000'. "to avoid divide by 0 error
  631      ton_bbl = '1'.
  632      DATA(ton_flg) = 'X'.
  633    ENDIF.
  634
  635  *     Calculate the values screen input/fetched (formula :- oil X conver
       sion|gas X conversion||exception=>TON:- oil / Conversion)
  636    IF spl_flg NE 'X'.
  637      LOOP AT it_quant INTO wa_quant.
  638        CASE sy-tabix.
  639          WHEN 1.  " 1 Opening inventory OVL:
  640            IF mode = 'CRE'.
  641              PERFORM cal_quant_cre USING sy-tabix.       "fetch values fr
       om internal table to calculate total value i.e 2 = 3 + 4.
  642
  643              wa_quant-bbl = opn_sto_o = q_val1_o + q_val2_o.
  644
  645            ELSEIF mode NE 'CRE'.
  646              CLEAR opn_sto_o.
  647              wa_quant-bbl = opn_sto_o = wa_final_qi_o-ost_tnk + wa_final_
       qi_o-ost_pip.   "When 1 = 2 + 3.
  648
  649            ENDIF.
  650
  651            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_quant-bbl * bbl_mmbbl.
  652            wa_quant-ton   = wa_quant-bbl / ton_bbl.
  653            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
  654
  655            wa_quant-mmbtu = 0 * mmscm_mmbtu.
  656            wa_quant-mmboe = 0 * mmscm_mmboe.
  657            wa_quant-bcm   = 0 * mmscm_bcm.
  658
  659          WHEN 2.  " 1a Opening inventory OVL: Stock in Storage tanks/loca
       tions/FPSO
  660            wa_final_qi_o-ost_tnk = wa_quant-bbl.
  661  *           " N/A for Gas
  662
  663            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-ost_tnk * bbl_mmbbl.
  664            wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-ost_tnk / ton_bbl.
  665            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
  666
  667            wa_quant-mmbtu = 0 * mmscm_mmbtu.
  668            wa_quant-mmboe = 0 * mmscm_mmboe.
  669            wa_quant-bcm   = 0 * mmscm_bcm.
  670
  671          WHEN 3.  "1b Opening inventory OVL: Stock in pipelines/linefill
  672            wa_final_qi_o-ost_pip = wa_quant-bbl.
  673            " N/A for Gas
  674
  675            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-ost_pip * bbl_mmbbl.
  676            wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-ost_pip / ton_bbl.
  677            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
  678
  679            wa_quant-mmbtu = 0 * mmscm_mmbtu.
  680            wa_quant-mmboe = 0 * mmscm_mmboe.
  681            wa_quant-bcm   = 0 * mmscm_bcm.
  682
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
27.09.26 OCP      Include MZFIPLF01                             Page     16
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  683          WHEN 5. "2 OVL share of Production
  684            wa_final_qi_o-sh_prod = wa_quant-bbl.
  685            wa_final_qi_g-sh_prod = wa_quant-mmscm.
  686
  687            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-sh_prod * bbl_mmbbl.
  688            wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-sh_prod / ton_bbl.
  689            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
  690
  691            wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_final_qi_g-sh_prod * mmscm_mmbtu.
  692            wa_quant-mmboe = wa_final_qi_g-sh_prod * mmscm_mmboe.
  693            wa_quant-bcm   = wa_final_qi_g-sh_prod * mmscm_bcm.
  694
  695          WHEN 7. "3 Profit Oil/Gas:
  696            CLEAR : prf_oil_o,prf_oil_g.
  697
  698            IF mode = 'CRE'.
  699              PERFORM cal_quant_cre USING sy-tabix.
  700
  701              wa_quant-bbl   = prf_oil_o = q_val1_o + q_val2_o.
  702              wa_quant-mmscm = prf_oil_g = q_val1_g + q_val2_g.
  703
  704            ELSEIF mode NE 'CRE'.
  705              prf_oil_o = wa_quant-bbl   = wa_final_qi_o-sh_lift + wa_fina
       l_qi_o-sh_take.
  706              prf_oil_g = wa_quant-mmscm = wa_final_qi_g-sh_lift + wa_fina
       l_qi_g-sh_take.
  707
  708            ENDIF.
  709
  710            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_quant-bbl * bbl_mmbbl.
  711            wa_quant-ton   = wa_quant-bbl / ton_bbl.
  712            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
  713
  714            wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmbtu.
  715            wa_quant-mmboe = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmboe.
  716            wa_quant-bcm   = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_bcm.
  717
  718
  719          WHEN 8. "3a State/Govt. share-lifted by OVL
  720            wa_final_qi_o-sh_lift = wa_quant-bbl.
  721            wa_final_qi_g-sh_lift = wa_quant-mmscm.
  722
  723            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-sh_lift * bbl_mmbbl.
  724            wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-sh_lift / ton_bbl.
  725            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
  726
  727            wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_final_qi_g-sh_lift * mmscm_mmbtu.
  728            wa_quant-mmboe = wa_final_qi_g-sh_lift * mmscm_mmboe.
  729            wa_quant-bcm   = wa_final_qi_g-sh_lift * mmscm_bcm.
  730
  731
  732          WHEN 9. "3b State/Govt. share-taken by state in kind
  733            wa_final_qi_o-sh_take = wa_quant-bbl.
  734            wa_final_qi_g-sh_take = wa_quant-mmscm.
  735
  736            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-sh_take * bbl_mmbbl.
  737            wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-sh_take / ton_bbl.
  738            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
  739
  740            wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_final_qi_g-sh_take * mmscm_mmbtu.
  741            wa_quant-mmboe = wa_final_qi_g-sh_take * mmscm_mmboe.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
27.09.26 OCP      Include MZFIPLF01                             Page     17
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  742            wa_quant-bcm   = wa_final_qi_g-sh_take * mmscm_bcm.
  743
  744
  745          WHEN 11.  "4 OVL share of entitled production (entitlement) (2-3
       )
  746            wa_quant-bbl   = sh_ent_o = wa_final_qi_o-sh_prod - prf_oil_o.
         "     When 11 = 5 - 7
  747            wa_quant-mmscm = sh_ent_g = wa_final_qi_g-sh_prod - prf_oil_g.
  748
  749            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_quant-bbl * bbl_mmbbl.
  750            wa_quant-ton   = wa_quant-bbl / ton_bbl.
  751            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
  752
  753            wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmbtu.
  754            wa_quant-mmboe = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmboe.
  755            wa_quant-bcm   = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_bcm.
  756
  757            CLEAR: prf_oil_o,
  758                   prf_oil_g.
  759
  760
  761          WHEN 13. "5 Transit Loss & Flaring:
  762            IF mode = 'CRE'.
  763              PERFORM cal_quant_cre USING sy-tabix.  "fetch values from th
       e internal tables for further calculations
  764
  765              wa_quant-bbl   = trl_flr_o = q_val1_o + q_val2_o.
  766              wa_quant-mmscm = trl_flr_g = q_val1_g + q_val2_g.
  767
  768            ELSEIF mode NE 'CRE'.
  769
  770              wa_quant-bbl   = trl_flr_o = wa_final_qi_o-trn_los + wa_fina
       l_qi_o-fl_vent.  " When 13 = 14 + 15
  771              wa_quant-mmscm = trl_flr_g = wa_final_qi_g-trn_los + wa_fina
       l_qi_g-fl_vent.
  772
  773            ENDIF.
  774
  775            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_quant-bbl * bbl_mmbbl.
  776            wa_quant-ton   = wa_quant-bbl / ton_bbl.
  777            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
  778
  779            wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmbtu.
  780            wa_quant-mmboe = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmboe.
  781            wa_quant-bcm   = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_bcm.
  782
  783
  784          WHEN 14.    "5a Transit loss
  785            wa_final_qi_o-trn_los = wa_quant-bbl.
  786            wa_final_qi_g-trn_los = wa_quant-mmscm.
  787
  788            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-trn_los * bbl_mmbbl.
  789            wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-trn_los / ton_bbl.
  790            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
  791
  792            wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_final_qi_g-trn_los * mmscm_mmbtu.
  793            wa_quant-mmboe = wa_final_qi_g-trn_los * mmscm_mmboe.
  794            wa_quant-bcm   = wa_final_qi_g-trn_los * mmscm_bcm.
  795
  796
  797          WHEN 15. "5b Flaring/vented
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
27.09.26 OCP      Include MZFIPLF01                             Page     18
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  798            wa_final_qi_o-fl_vent = wa_quant-bbl.
  799            wa_final_qi_g-fl_vent = wa_quant-mmscm.
  800
  801            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-fl_vent * bbl_mmbbl.
  802            wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-fl_vent / ton_bbl.
  803            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
  804
  805            wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_final_qi_g-fl_vent * mmscm_mmbtu.
  806            wa_quant-mmboe = wa_final_qi_g-fl_vent * mmscm_mmboe.
  807            wa_quant-bcm   = wa_final_qi_g-fl_vent * mmscm_bcm.
  808
  809
  810          WHEN 17.  "6 Production quantity after transit loss & flaring (4
       -5)
  811            wa_quant-bbl   = atrl_flr_o = sh_ent_o - trl_flr_o .
         " When 17 = 11 - 13
  812            wa_quant-mmscm = atrl_flr_g = sh_ent_g - trl_flr_g .
  813
  814            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_quant-bbl * bbl_mmbbl.
  815            wa_quant-ton   = wa_quant-bbl / ton_bbl.
  816            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
  817
  818            wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmbtu.
  819            wa_quant-mmboe = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmboe.
  820            wa_quant-bcm   = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_bcm.
  821
  822
  823          WHEN 19. "7 Internal consumption:
  824            IF mode = 'CRE'.
  825              PERFORM cal_quant_cre USING sy-tabix.
  826
  827              wa_quant-bbl   = int_con_o = q_val1_o + q_val2_o.
  828              wa_quant-mmscm = int_con_g = q_val1_g + q_val2_g.
  829
  830            ELSEIF mode NE 'CRE'.
  831              wa_quant-bbl   = int_con_o = wa_final_qi_o-prd_act + wa_fina
       l_qi_o-others .    "When 19 = 20 + 21
  832              wa_quant-mmscm = int_con_g = wa_final_qi_g-prd_act + wa_fina
       l_qi_g-others .
  833
  834            ENDIF.
  835
  836            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_quant-bbl * bbl_mmbbl.
  837            wa_quant-ton   = wa_quant-bbl / ton_bbl.
  838            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
  839
  840            wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmbtu.
  841            wa_quant-mmboe = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmboe.
  842            wa_quant-bcm   = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_bcm.
  843
  844
  845          WHEN 20. "7a Production activity
  846            wa_final_qi_o-prd_act = wa_quant-bbl.
  847            wa_final_qi_g-prd_act = wa_quant-mmscm.
  848
  849            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-prd_act * bbl_mmbbl.
  850            wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-prd_act / ton_bbl.
  851            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
  852
  853            wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_final_qi_g-prd_act * mmscm_mmbtu.
  854            wa_quant-mmboe = wa_final_qi_g-prd_act * mmscm_mmboe.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
27.09.26 OCP      Include MZFIPLF01                             Page     19
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  855            wa_quant-bcm   = wa_final_qi_g-prd_act * mmscm_bcm.
  856
  857
  858          WHEN 21. "7b Others
  859            wa_final_qi_o-others = wa_quant-bbl.
  860            wa_final_qi_g-others = wa_quant-mmscm.
  861
  862            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-others * bbl_mmbbl.
  863            wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-others / ton_bbl.
  864            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
  865
  866            wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_final_qi_g-others * mmscm_mmbtu.
  867            wa_quant-mmboe = wa_final_qi_g-others * mmscm_mmboe.
  868            wa_quant-bcm   = wa_final_qi_g-others * mmscm_bcm.
  869
  870
  871          WHEN 23. "8 Net production quantity available (6-7)
  872            wa_quant-bbl   = net_prd_o = atrl_flr_o - int_con_o .
          " When 23 = 17 - 19
  873            wa_quant-mmscm = net_prd_g = atrl_flr_g - int_con_g .
  874
  875            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_quant-bbl * bbl_mmbbl.
  876            wa_quant-ton   = wa_quant-bbl / ton_bbl.
  877            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
  878
  879            wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmbtu.
  880            wa_quant-mmboe = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmboe.
  881            wa_quant-bcm   = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_bcm.
  882
  883
  884          WHEN 25. "9 Closing Stock:
  885            IF mode = 'CRE'.
  886              PERFORM cal_quant_cre USING sy-tabix.
  887
  888              wa_quant-bbl = clo_sto_o = q_val1_o + q_val2_o.
  889
  890            ELSEIF mode NE 'CRE'.
  891              wa_quant-bbl = clo_sto_o = wa_final_qi_o-cst_tnk + wa_final_
       qi_o-cst_pip .      "When 25 = 26 + 27
  892
  893            ENDIF.
  894
  895            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_quant-bbl * bbl_mmbbl.
  896            wa_quant-ton   = wa_quant-bbl / ton_bbl.
  897            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
  898
  899            wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmbtu.
  900            wa_quant-mmboe = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmboe.
  901            wa_quant-bcm   = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_bcm.
  902
  903
  904          WHEN 26. "9a Stock in Storage tanks/locations/FPSO
  905            wa_final_qi_o-cst_tnk = wa_quant-bbl.
  906
  907            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-cst_tnk * bbl_mmbbl.
  908            wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-cst_tnk / ton_bbl.
  909            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
  910
  911            wa_quant-mmbtu = 0 * mmscm_mmbtu.
  912            wa_quant-mmboe = 0 * mmscm_mmboe.
  913            wa_quant-bcm   = 0 * mmscm_bcm.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
27.09.26 OCP      Include MZFIPLF01                             Page     20
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  914
  915
  916          WHEN 27. "9b Stock in pipelines/linefill
  917            wa_final_qi_o-cst_pip = wa_quant-bbl.
  918            "N/A for gas
  919
  920            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-cst_pip * bbl_mmbbl.
  921            wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-cst_pip / ton_bbl.
  922            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
  923
  924            wa_quant-mmbtu = 0 * mmscm_mmbtu.
  925            wa_quant-mmboe = 0 * mmscm_mmboe.
  926            wa_quant-bcm   = 0 * mmscm_bcm.
  927
  928
  929          WHEN 29. "10 Adj. for Changes in Inventories/Other adj.
  930            wa_final_qi_o-ch_invt = chg_inv_o = wa_quant-bbl.
  931            wa_final_qi_g-ch_invt = chg_inv_g = wa_quant-mmscm.
  932
  933            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-ch_invt * bbl_mmbbl.
  934            wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-ch_invt / ton_bbl.
  935            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
  936
  937            wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_final_qi_g-ch_invt * mmscm_mmbtu.
  938            wa_quant-mmboe = wa_final_qi_g-ch_invt * mmscm_mmboe.
  939            wa_quant-bcm   = wa_final_qi_g-ch_invt * mmscm_bcm.
  940
  941
  942          WHEN 31.  "11 Net quantity available for sale (8-9-10+1+3a)
  943            wa_quant-bbl   = net_prd_o - clo_sto_o - chg_inv_o + opn_sto_o
        + wa_final_qi_o-sh_lift.
  944            wa_quant-mmscm = net_prd_g - chg_inv_g + wa_final_qi_g-sh_lift
       .
  945            "When 31 = 23 - 25 - 29 + 1 + 8.
  946            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_quant-bbl * bbl_mmbbl.
  947            wa_quant-ton   = wa_quant-bbl / ton_bbl.
  948            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
  949
  950            wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmbtu.
  951            wa_quant-mmboe = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmboe.
  952            wa_quant-bcm   = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_bcm.
  953
  954
  955          WHEN 33. "12 Overlift/(underlift) quantity
  956            IF mode = 'CRE'.
  957              PERFORM cal_quant_cre USING sy-tabix.
  958
  959              wa_final_qi_o-ovr_und = wa_quant-bbl = ( q_val2_o ) - ( net_
       prd_o - clo_sto_o - chg_inv_o + opn_sto_o + wa_final_qi_o-sh_lift ).
  960              "N/A for gas
  961
  962            ELSEIF mode NE 'CRE'.
  963              wa_final_qi_o-ovr_und = wa_quant-bbl = ( wa_final_qi_o-tot_s
       al ) - ( net_prd_o - clo_sto_o - chg_inv_o + opn_sto_o + wa_final_qi_o-s
       h_lift ).        " 35 = 36 - 33
  964              "N/A for gas
  965            ENDIF.
  966
  967            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-ovr_und * bbl_mmbbl.
  968            wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-ovr_und / ton_bbl.
  969            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
27.09.26 OCP      Include MZFIPLF01                             Page     21
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  970
  971            wa_quant-mmbtu = 0 * mmscm_mmbtu.
  972            wa_quant-mmboe = 0 * mmscm_mmboe.
  973            wa_quant-bcm   = 0 * mmscm_bcm.
  974
  975
  976          WHEN 35.  "13 Total Sales quantity
  977            wa_final_qi_o-tot_sal = wa_quant-bbl.
  978            wa_final_qi_g-tot_sal = wa_quant-mmscm.
  979
  980            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-tot_sal * bbl_mmbbl.
  981            wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-tot_sal / ton_bbl.
  982            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
  983
  984            wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_final_qi_g-tot_sal * mmscm_mmbtu.
  985            wa_quant-mmboe = wa_final_qi_g-tot_sal * mmscm_mmboe.
  986            wa_quant-bcm   = wa_final_qi_g-tot_sal * mmscm_bcm.
  987
  988          WHEN 37.  "14 Total Sales quantity-net of state share (13-3a)
  989            READ TABLE it_quant INTO DATA(wa_quant_z1) INDEX 8.
  990            READ TABLE it_quant INTO DATA(wa_quant_z2) INDEX 35.
  991            wa_quant-bbl   = wa_quant_z2-bbl - wa_quant_z1-bbl.
  992            wa_quant-mmscm = wa_quant_z2-mmscm - wa_quant_z1-mmscm.
  993
  994            wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_quant_z2-mmbbl - wa_quant_z1-mmbbl.
  995            wa_quant-ton   = wa_quant_z2-ton - wa_quant_z1-ton.
  996            wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant_z2-mmt - wa_quant_z1-mmt.
  997
  998            wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_quant_z2-mmbtu - wa_quant_z1-mmbtu.
  999            wa_quant-mmboe = wa_quant_z2-mmboe - wa_quant_z1-mmboe.
 1000            wa_quant-bcm   = wa_quant_z2-bcm - wa_quant_z1-bcm.
 1001
 1002            CLEAR : wa_quant_z1,wa_quant_z2.
 1003        ENDCASE.
 1004
 1005
 1006        IF ( mode NE 'CRE' ) OR ( mode NE 'CHA' ).
 1007          IF sy-ucomm = 'SAVE'.
 1008
 1009            IF wa_quant-io_flag CA '1'.        "Check Mandatory fields for
        OIL column
 1010              IF wa_quant-bbl = ' '.
 1011                CLEAR : sy-ucomm,lv_msg1.
 1012                CONCATENATE 'Field' wa_quant-data 'of BBL column cannot be
        blank' INTO lv_msg1 SEPARATED BY space.
 1013                MESSAGE lv_msg1 TYPE 'E'.
 1014              ENDIF.
 1015            ENDIF.
 1016
 1017            IF wa_quant-io_flag CA '2'.         "Check Mandatory fields fo
       r GAS column
 1018              IF wa_quant-mmscm = ' '.
 1019                CLEAR : sy-ucomm,lv_msg1.
 1020                CONCATENATE 'Field' wa_quant-data 'of MMSCM column cannot
       be blank' INTO lv_msg1 SEPARATED BY space.
 1021                MESSAGE lv_msg1 TYPE 'E'.
 1022              ENDIF.
 1023            ENDIF.
 1024
 1025          ENDIF.
 1026
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
27.09.26 OCP      Include MZFIPLF01                             Page     22
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 1027          IF wa_quant-bbl IS NOT INITIAL.  "round off all values to 3 deci
       mals
 1028            wa_quant-bbl   = round( val = wa_quant-bbl   dec = '3' ).
 1029            wa_quant-mmbbl = round( val = wa_quant-mmbbl dec = '3' ).
 1030            wa_quant-mmt   = round( val = wa_quant-mmt   dec = '3' ).
 1031            wa_quant-ton   = round( val = wa_quant-ton   dec = '3' ).
 1032          ENDIF.
 1033
 1034          IF wa_quant-mmscm IS NOT INITIAL. "round off all values to 3 dec
       imals
 1035            wa_quant-mmscm = round( val = wa_quant-mmscm dec = '3' ).
 1036            wa_quant-mmbtu = round( val = wa_quant-mmbtu dec = '3' ).
 1037            wa_quant-mmboe = round( val = wa_quant-mmboe dec = '3' ).
 1038            wa_quant-bcm   = round( val = wa_quant-bcm   dec = '3' ).
 1039          ENDIF.
 1040
 1041          MODIFY it_quant FROM wa_quant TRANSPORTING bbl mmscm mmbbl mmt t
       on mmbtu mmboe bcm.
 1042          CLEAR wa_quant.
 1043
 1044        ENDIF.
 1045
 1046      ENDLOOP.
 1047    ENDIF.
 1048
 1049    IF ton_flg = 'X'.
 1050      CLEAR ton_bbl.
 1051    ENDIF.
 1052    CLEAR ton_flg.
 1053
 1054    CLEAR : prf_oil_o, sh_ent_o, trl_flr_o, atrl_flr_o, int_con_o,
 1055            prf_oil_g, sh_ent_g, trl_flr_g, atrl_flr_g, int_con_g,
 1056            net_prd_o, opn_sto_o, clo_sto_o, chg_inv_o, chg_inv_o,
 1057            net_prd_g,                                  chg_inv_g.
 1058
 1059
 1060
 1061  ENDFORM."CAL_QUANT

       nal tables for further calculations

 1206  FORM fetch_quant.  "Fetch saved/created data from tables
 1207
 1208    DATA s_period_low_1(6) TYPE c.

 1240    ENDIF.

       "get Quant Oil data

       "get Quant Oil data

 1272                AND pro_nam = p_proj
 1273                AND fisyear = p_year
 1274                AND zperiod IN ( s_period_low_1 , s_period_low_2 ).

       "get Quant Gas data

        as read only/available as output field
 1994                                 CHANGING lt_celltab.
 1995          ENDIF.
 1996        ENDIF.

 1999        MODIFY it_prof_prd INDEX l_index.
 2000

 2007  FORM fill_celltab_prof_prd USING VALUE(p_mode) VALUE(io_flag2)
 2008                    CHANGING pt_celltab TYPE lvc_t_styl.
 2009
 2010    DATA : ls_celltab TYPE lvc_t_styl WITH HEADER LINE.
 2011
 2012    CLEAR l_mode.
 2013    CLEAR l_mode2.
 2014
 2015    IF p_mode EQ 'RW'.
 2016      l_mode = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_enabled.
 2017    ELSE.
 2018      l_mode = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.
 2019    ENDIF.
 2020
 2021    ls_celltab-fieldname = 'PER'.
 2022    ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.   "first column
        as read only
 2023
 2024    IF io_flag2 CA '0'.
 2025    ENDIF.
 2026    INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.
 2027
 2028    ls_celltab-fieldname = 'OILNG'.
 2029    ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.   "second colum
       n as read only
 2030    INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.
 2031
 2032    ls_celltab-fieldname = 'OIL'.                            "third column
        as read only/read and write field
 2033    IF io_flag2 CA '2'.
 2034      ls_celltab-style = l_mode.
 2035    ELSE.
 2036      ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.
 2037    ENDIF.
 2038    INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.
 2039
 2040    ls_celltab-fieldname = 'GAS'.                           "fourth column
        as read only/read and write field
 2041    IF io_flag2 CA '3'.
 2042      ls_celltab-style = l_mode.
 2043    ELSE.
 2044      ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.
 2045    ENDIF.
 2046    INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.
 2047
 2048  ENDFORM."fill_celltab_prof_prd

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 2170
 2171      DO 6 TIMES.
 2172        APPEND wa_prof_prd TO it_prof_prd.
 2173      ENDDO.

 2176        MOVE-CORRESPONDING wa_prof_prd2 TO wa_prof_prd.
 2177        APPEND wa_prof_prd TO it_prof_prd.

 2183
 2184      IF wa_prof_prd-oil CA lv_spl.

       ' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.

       ' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
 2193        EXIT.
 2194      ENDIF.

 2196      CASE sy-tabix.
 2197        WHEN 1 ."GROSS PRODUCTION
 2198          wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
 2199          wa_prd_oil-sh_prod = wa_prof_prd-oil.
 2200          wa_prd_gas-sh_prod = wa_prof_prd-gas.
 2201

 2207        WHEN 3 ."OVLs ENTITLEMENT (AFTER PROFIT OIL))

 2209          wa_prd_oil-sh_entl = wa_prof_prd-oil.
 2210          wa_prd_gas-sh_entl = wa_prof_prd-gas.
 2211
 2212        WHEN 4 ."ADD/(LESS):OVERLIFT QTY/(UNDER LIFT)
 2213          wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
 2214          wa_prd_oil-ovr_und = wa_prof_prd-oil.
 2215          wa_prd_gas-ovr_und = wa_prof_prd-gas.
 2216
 2217        WHEN 5 ."ADD/(LESS): CHANGES IN INVENTORIES
 2218          wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
 2219          wa_prd_oil-chg_inv = wa_prof_prd-oil.
 2220          wa_prd_gas-chg_inv = wa_prof_prd-gas.
 2221
 2222        WHEN 6 ."LESS: INTERNAL CONSUMPTION/RE-INJECTION IN RESERVOIR
 2223          wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
 2224          wa_prd_oil-int_con = wa_prof_prd-oil.
 2225          wa_prd_gas-int_con = wa_prof_prd-gas.
 2226
 2227        WHEN 7 ."REVENUE FROM CRUDE OIL
 2228          wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
27.09.26 OCP      Include MZFIPLF01                             Page     44
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 2229          wa_prd_oil-rev_oil = wa_prof_prd-oil.
 2230          wa_prd_gas-rev_oil = wa_prof_prd-gas.
 2231
 2232        WHEN 8 ."REVENUE FROM NATURAL GAS
 2233          wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
 2234          wa_prd_oil-rev_gas = wa_prof_prd-oil.
 2235          wa_prd_gas-rev_gas = wa_prof_prd-gas.
 2236
 2237        WHEN 9 ."Less: Profit Oil paid in cash (applicable for Sakhalin)
 2238          wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
 2239          wa_prd_oil-prfoilc = wa_prof_prd-oil.
 2240          wa_prd_gas-prfoilc = wa_prof_prd-gas.
 2241

 2243          wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
 2244          wa_prd_oil-sal_vap = wa_prof_prd-oil.
 2245          wa_prd_gas-sal_vap = wa_prof_prd-gas.
 2246
 2247        WHEN 11."OTHER OPERATING REVENUE
 2248          wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
 2249          wa_prd_oil-oth_rev = wa_prof_prd-oil.

 2677          wa_prof_prd-gas = wa_prd_gas-oechin1 .

 2683          wa_prof_prd-gas = wa_prd_gas-oe_ovun .

 2685
 2686        WHEN 41."OTHER INCOME
 2687

 2689          wa_prof_prd-gas = wa_prd_gas-oth_oth .
 2690          wa_prof_prd-oilng = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
 2691
 2692        WHEN 42."FINANCE COST
 2693

 2695          wa_prof_prd-gas = wa_prd_gas-oth_fin .

 3037  FORM calc_0311.           "calculate fetched/input data
 3038
 3039    IF NOT grid1 IS INITIAL.
 3040      CALL METHOD grid1->check_changed_data.
 3041    ENDIF.
 3042
 3043    wa_pnl_oth-company = p_company.
 3044    wa_pnl_oth-company = p_company.
 3045
 3046    wa_pnl_oth-bukrs = p_comp.
 3047    wa_pnl_oth-bukrs = p_comp.
 3048
 3049    wa_pnl_oth-pro_nam = p_proj.
 3050    wa_pnl_oth-pro_nam = p_proj.
 3051
 3052    wa_pnl_oth-fisyear = p_year.
 3053    wa_pnl_oth-fisyear = p_year.
 3054
 3055    wa_pnl_oth-zperiod = s_period_low.
 3056    wa_pnl_oth-zperiod = s_period_low.
 3057
 3058    DATA : tot_inc(10)   TYPE c,
 3059           tot_exp(10)   TYPE c,
 3060           prof_btax(10) TYPE c,
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
27.09.26 OCP      Include MZFIPLF01                             Page     58
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 3061           prof_atax(10) TYPE c.
 3062
 3063    LOOP AT it_oth_proj INTO wa_oth_proj.
 3064
 3065      IF wa_oth_proj-vals CA lv_spl AND sy-tabix NE 1.
 3066        CLEAR sy-ucomm.
 3067        MESSAGE 'Special character in amount field is not allowed' TYPE 'W
       ' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
 3068        EXIT.
 3069      ENDIF.
 3070
 3071      CASE sy-tabix.
 3072        WHEN 1.
 3073
 3074        WHEN 2.
 3075          wa_pnl_oth-rev_opr = wa_oth_proj-vals.
 3076        WHEN 3.
 3077          wa_pnl_oth-oth_inc = wa_oth_proj-vals.
 3078        WHEN 4.
 3079          tot_inc = wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-rev_opr + wa_pnl_oth-oth
       _inc.
 3080        WHEN 5.
 3081        WHEN 6.
 3082          wa_pnl_oth-chg_inv = wa_oth_proj-vals.
 3083        WHEN 7.
 3084          wa_pnl_oth-ovr_und = wa_oth_proj-vals.
 3085        WHEN 8.
 3086          wa_pnl_oth-pts_de = wa_oth_proj-vals.
 3087        WHEN 9.
 3088          wa_pnl_oth-exp_cst = wa_oth_proj-vals.
 3089        WHEN 10.
 3090          wa_pnl_oth-fin_cst = wa_oth_proj-vals.
 3091        WHEN 11.
 3092          wa_pnl_oth-dda = wa_oth_proj-vals.
 3093        WHEN 12.
 3094          wa_pnl_oth-prov_of = wa_oth_proj-vals.
 3095        WHEN 13.
 3096          wa_pnl_oth-oth_exp = wa_oth_proj-vals.
 3097        WHEN 14.
 3098          tot_exp = wa_oth_proj-vals =  wa_pnl_oth-chg_inv + wa_pnl_oth-ov
       r_und
 3099                                      + wa_pnl_oth-pts_de  + wa_pnl_oth-ex
       p_cst
 3100                                      + wa_pnl_oth-fin_cst + wa_pnl_oth-dd
       a
 3101                                      + wa_pnl_oth-prov_of + wa_pnl_oth-ot
       h_exp.
 3102
 3103        WHEN 15.
 3104        WHEN 16.
 3105          prof_btax = wa_oth_proj-vals = tot_inc - tot_exp.
 3106        WHEN 17.
 3107        WHEN 18.
 3108          wa_pnl_oth-inc_tax = wa_oth_proj-vals.
 3109        WHEN 19.
 3110        WHEN 20.
 3111          wa_oth_proj-vals = prof_btax - wa_pnl_oth-inc_tax.
 3112
 3113      ENDCASE.
 3114      MODIFY it_oth_proj FROM wa_oth_proj TRANSPORTING vals.
 3115      CLEAR wa_oth_proj.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
27.09.26 OCP      Include MZFIPLF01                             Page     59
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 3116    ENDLOOP.
 3117
 3118
 3119  ENDFORM."CALC_0311

 3121  FORM change_oth_proj.    "get previously filled data
 3122
 3123    IF NOT grid1 IS INITIAL.
 3124      CALL METHOD grid1->check_changed_data.
 3125    ENDIF.
 3126
 3127    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zpnl_oth_proj
 3128         INTO @DATA(chk_oth_proj)
 3129         WHERE company = @p_company
 3130           AND bukrs   = @p_comp
 3131           AND pro_nam = @p_proj
 3132           AND fisyear = @p_year
 3133           AND zperiod = @s_period_low.
 3134
 3135    IF chk_oth_proj IS NOT INITIAL.
 3136      MOVE-CORRESPONDING chk_oth_proj TO wa_pnl_oth.
 3137    ENDIF.
 3138
 3139    DATA : tot_inc(10)   TYPE c,
 3140           tot_exp(10)   TYPE c,
 3141           prof_btax(10) TYPE c,
 3142           prof_atax(10) TYPE c.
 3143
 3144    LOOP AT it_oth_proj INTO wa_oth_proj.
 3145
 3146      CASE sy-tabix.
 3147        WHEN 1.
 3148
 3149        WHEN 2.
 3150          wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-rev_opr.
 3151        WHEN 3.
 3152          wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-oth_inc.
 3153        WHEN 4.
 3154          tot_inc = wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-rev_opr + wa_pnl_oth-oth
       _inc.
 3155        WHEN 5.
 3156        WHEN 6.
 3157          wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-chg_inv.
 3158        WHEN 7.
 3159          wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-ovr_und.
 3160        WHEN 8.
 3161          wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-pts_de .
 3162        WHEN 9.
 3163          wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-exp_cst.
 3164        WHEN 10.
 3165          wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-fin_cst.
 3166        WHEN 11.
 3167          wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-dda    .
 3168        WHEN 12.
 3169          wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-prov_of.
 3170        WHEN 13.
 3171          wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-oth_exp.
 3172        WHEN 14.
 3173          tot_exp = wa_oth_proj-vals =  wa_pnl_oth-chg_inv + wa_pnl_oth-ov
       r_und
 3174                                      + wa_pnl_oth-pts_de  + wa_pnl_oth-ex
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
27.09.26 OCP      Include MZFIPLF01                             Page     60
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

       p_cst
 3175                                      + wa_pnl_oth-fin_cst + wa_pnl_oth-dd
       a
 3176                                      + wa_pnl_oth-prov_of + wa_pnl_oth-ot
       h_exp.
 3177
 3178        WHEN 15.
 3179        WHEN 16.
 3180          prof_btax = wa_oth_proj-vals = tot_inc - tot_exp.
 3181        WHEN 17.
 3182        WHEN 18.
 3183          wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-inc_tax.
 3184        WHEN 19.
 3185        WHEN 20.
 3186          wa_oth_proj-vals = prof_btax - wa_pnl_oth-inc_tax.
 3187
 3188      ENDCASE.
 3189
 3190      MODIFY it_oth_proj FROM wa_oth_proj TRANSPORTING vals.
 3191      CLEAR wa_oth_proj.
 3192

 3197  FORM save_oth_proj.       "save data into database tables
 3198
 3199    IF NOT grid1 IS INITIAL.
 3200      CALL METHOD grid1->check_changed_data.
 3201    ENDIF.
 3202
 3203
 3204    IF mode EQ 'FRE'.
 3205      IF wa_pnl_oth-approve_by IS INITIAL AND wa_pnl_oth-approve_on IS INI
       TIAL.
 3206        MESSAGE 'Data is not approved yet' TYPE 'E'.
 3207      ENDIF.
 3208    ENDIF.
 3209
 3210    PERFORM popup_save.
 3211
 3212    IF ( mode NE 'DIS' ) .
 3213      IF ans = '1'.
 3214        IF wa_pnl_oth IS NOT INITIAL .
 3215
 3216          IF mode = 'CRE'.
 3217            wa_pnl_oth-created_by = sy-uname.
 3218            wa_pnl_oth-created_on = sy-datum.
 3219
 3220          ELSEIF mode = 'CHA'.
 3221            wa_pnl_oth-changed_by = sy-uname.
 3222            wa_pnl_oth-changed_on = sy-datum.
 3223          ELSEIF mode = 'APP'.
 3224            wa_pnl_oth-approve_by = sy-uname.
 3225            wa_pnl_oth-approve_on = sy-datum.
 3226          ELSEIF mode = 'FRE'.
 3227            wa_pnl_oth-freezed_by = sy-uname.
 3228            wa_pnl_oth-freezed_on = sy-datum.
 3229
 3230          ELSEIF mode = 'APF'.
 3231            wa_pnl_oth-approved_by_rp = sy-uname.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
27.09.26 OCP      Include MZFIPLF01                             Page     61
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 3232            wa_pnl_oth-approved_on_rp = sy-datum.
 3233          ENDIF.
 3234
 3235          MODIFY zpnl_oth_proj FROM wa_pnl_oth.
 3236          CLEAR : wa_pnl_oth.
 3237
 3238          MESSAGE 'Data saved successfully.' TYPE 'S'.
 3239
 3240        ENDIF.
 3241      ENDIF.
 3242      CLEAR ans.
 3243    ENDIF.
 3244
 3245  ENDFORM."SAVE_OTH_PROJ

 7015          wa_final_bar-oil3 = net_atax_oil3 + imploss_oil3 .
 7016          wa_final_bar-oil4 = net_atax_oil4 + imploss_oil4 .
 7017
 7018          wa_final_bar-gas1 = net_atax_gas1 + imploss_gas1 .
 7019          wa_final_bar-gas2 = net_atax_gas2 + imploss_gas2 .
 7020          wa_final_bar-gas3 = net_atax_gas3 + imploss_gas3 .
 7021          wa_final_bar-gas4 = net_atax_gas4 + imploss_gas4 .
 7022
 7023          MODIFY it_final_bar FROM wa_final_bar TRANSPORTING total1 total2
        total3 total4
 7024                                                              oil1   oil2
         oil3   oil4
 7025                                                              gas1   gas2
         gas3   gas4.
 7026          CLEAR wa_final_bar.
 7027
 7028      ENDCASE.
 7029
 7030    ENDLOOP.
 7031
 7032    CLEAR : wa_final_bar2, wa_final_bar3.
 7033
 7034  *Rounding off every amount to 2 decimals
 7035    LOOP AT it_final_bar INTO wa_final_bar.
 7036
 7037      wa_final_bar1-per = wa_final_bar-per.
 7038
 7039      wa_final_bar1-total1 = round( val = wa_final_bar-total1 dec = '2' )
       .
 7040      wa_final_bar1-total2 = round( val = wa_final_bar-total2 dec = '2' )
       .
 7041      wa_final_bar1-total3 = round( val = wa_final_bar-total3 dec = '2' )
       .
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
27.09.26 OCP      Include MZFIPLF01                             Page    140
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 7042      wa_final_bar1-total4 = round( val = wa_final_bar-total4 dec = '2' )
       .
 7043
 7044      wa_final_bar1-oil1 = round( val = wa_final_bar-oil1 dec = '2' ) .
 7045      wa_final_bar1-oil2 = round( val = wa_final_bar-oil2 dec = '2' ) .
 7046      wa_final_bar1-oil3 = round( val = wa_final_bar-oil3 dec = '2' ) .
 7047      wa_final_bar1-oil4 = round( val = wa_final_bar-oil4 dec = '2' ) .
 7048
 7049      wa_final_bar1-gas1 = round( val = wa_final_bar-gas1 dec = '2' ) .
 7050      wa_final_bar1-gas2 = round( val = wa_final_bar-gas2 dec = '2' ) .
 7051      wa_final_bar1-gas3 = round( val = wa_final_bar-gas3 dec = '2' ) .
 7052      wa_final_bar1-gas4 = round( val = wa_final_bar-gas4 dec = '2' ) .
 7053
 7054  *-------------------" Changes begins by Aparna on 11.02.2023 "----------
       ------------*
 7055  *BREAK-POINT.
 7056      SELECT SINGLE *
 7057      FROM   zfi_pl_var_prd
 7058      INTO   @DATA(wa_pl_var_prd)
 7059             WHERE per    = @wa_final_bar1-per
 7060             AND comp = @p_comp1
 7061             AND   period = @s_period-low
 7062        AND period2 = @s_period_low2
 7063        AND fiscal = @p_year1
 7064        AND proj = @p_proj1-low.
 7065      IF sy-subrc = 0.
 7066
 7067        wa_final_bar1-remark = wa_pl_var_prd-remark.
 7068
 7069      ENDIF.
 7070  *-------------------" Changes ended by Aparna on 11.02.2023 "-----------
       ------------*
 7071
 7072      APPEND wa_final_bar1 TO it_final_bar1.
 7073
 7074      CLEAR: wa_final_bar,wa_final_bar1.
 7075
 7076    ENDLOOP.
 7077
 7078  ENDFORM."CRE_PRD_PROJ.

 7173                         WHERE bukrs = p_comp1.

       fs_header-info SEPARATED BY space.
 7178    APPEND fs_header TO int_header.
 7179    CLEAR fs_header.

 7181    SELECT txtjv jvnam FROM zfi_pnl_proj INTO TABLE it_proj
 7182                         WHERE jvnam = p_proj1-low.
 7183
 7184    READ TABLE it_proj INTO DATA(wa_proj) WITH KEY jvnam = p_proj1-low.
 7185    fs_header-typ = 'S'.

       eader-info SEPARATED BY space.
 7187    APPEND fs_header TO int_header.
 7188    CLEAR fs_header.
 7189
 7190    fs_header-typ = 'S'.
 7191    APPEND fs_header TO int_header.
 7192    CLEAR fs_header.
 7193
 7194    fs_header-typ = 'S'.
 7195    CONCATENATE '(Amount in' 'US$ Mn.)' INTO fs_header-info SEPARATED BY s
       pace.
 7196    APPEND fs_header TO int_header.
 7197    CLEAR fs_header.

 7200      EXPORTING
 7201        it_list_commentary = int_header.
 7202
 7203  ENDFORM."TOP_OF_PAGE_BAR

 7205  FORM map_period USING VALUE(per_low).   "Get periods according to input
       parameters to pass in database tables
 7206    REFRESH s_peri[].
 7207    CLEAR : s_period_low,s_period_high,s_peri.
 7208
 7209    s_peri-sign   = 'I'.
 7210    s_peri-option = 'BT'.
 7211
 7212    IF per_low = 'Q1'.
 7213      s_period_low  = '01'.
 7214      s_period_high = '03'.
 7215    ELSEIF per_low = 'Q2'.
 7216      s_period_low  = '04'.
 7217      s_period_high = '06'.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
27.09.26 OCP      Include MZFIPLF01                             Page    143
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 7218    ELSEIF per_low = 'H1'.
 7219      s_period_low  = '01'.
 7220      s_period_high = '06'.
 7221    ELSEIF per_low = 'Q3'.
 7222      s_period_low  = '07'.
 7223      s_period_high = '09'.
 7224    ELSEIF per_low = '9M'.
 7225      s_period_low  = '01'.
 7226      s_period_high = '09'.
 7227    ELSEIF per_low = 'Q4'.
 7228      s_period_low  = '10'.
 7229      s_period_high = '16'.
 7230    ELSEIF per_low = 'ANNUAL'.
 7231      s_period_low  = '01'.
 7232      s_period_high = '16'.
 7233    ENDIF.
 7234
 7235    s_peri-low  = s_period_low.
 7236    s_peri-high = s_period_high.
 7237
 7238    APPEND s_peri.
 7239
 7240  ENDFORM."MAP_PERIOD
